**Three things this block fixes that a bigger number would not.** **(1) It balances**: round 2's first pasted block printed `dual 903 / sole 151` against rosters of `998` and `1081` — `903 + 151 = 1054`, which exhausts neither, and a spelling partition that does not exhaust its roster is not a partition. ```

**The claim the rule actually supports, stated at its true strength**: the left-token rule dissolves **70 of 299** zero-homed id-instances and **seven whole records** — it does NOT take any named family to zero by itself, and the family it was said to zero is the one carrying the residue §X.1-v3 never admitted. The corrected reading is kept, not the corrected number alone: **an alias rule shrinks a residue; only a partition homes one**, which is why §X.1-v4 exists and why §Z's dependent sentence now reads *dissolves most of* rather than *makes closable at all*. The same shape recurs on `·`/`/`-joined heads — ⟨cmd⟩ `grep -n 'D-m10' fr-DarkModeToggle.md` → `96:| **N-10 · D-m9/D-m10** | The 5rem `--toggle-size` default has never rendered …`, whose banked head **`N-10` is already booked at §C.H** — the first token of a `·`/`/`-joined head is the banked id, the rest are cross-reference axes. g19 carries the rule INLINE, now beside the fourth axis (record-qualification, §3 g19); without both the set-difference is non-empty by construction and the gate can never close — F-W8's G15 runnability lesson (R2-7.2) in this wave's own idiom.

### §C.D — The paper-search family

- **fr-PaperSearchModal PSM-1 (≡ fr-PaperSearch D-1/L-1/C-B1 ≡ fr-PaperSearchDropdown B-1 ≡ fr-PaperSearchInput C-1 ≡ fr-MobileFloatingToc B-1)** — BLOCKER, **the head of four same-commit riders**. Every CSS rule governing the family is orphaned by Vue scoping (the only `<style scoped>` lives in `PaperSearch.vue:41`; the modal subTree is a Teleport vnode, the dropdown a two-root Fragment, the input's children never carry the hash) — the modal ships unstyled to `<body>`. Cure: mechanical rule-move against **THE SEAT-ENUMERATED SET (R-5, verbatim)**: 48 rule blocks = 40 selector + 8 transition, of which 5 live (:43, :47, :58, :199, :204) and 43 dead — "F.W3/W4's mechanical rule-move takes THIS enumerated set" (the 49/5/44 recount is the kill of a kill, K-10). Provenance: refactor casualty at `ffba307`. ⟨PaperSearchModal; PaperSearch; PaperSearchDropdown; PaperSearchInput; MobileFloatingToc⟩ → g1, g13.
- **fr-PaperSearchInput MISS-LC1** — MAJOR, **mandatory rider on PSM-1**: the corpus's OWN prescribed cure orphans five cross-SFC shared hooks (`paper-search-action-btn` emitted by Input AND Modal; `-result`/`-badge`/`-number`/`-label` by Dropdown AND Modal) while both repair plans assign each class to exactly ONE child. Cure: shared hooks go in a non-scoped or shared home (or the markup moves with them) — never one-child-per-class. Same commit as PSM-1. ⟨PaperSearchInput⟩ → g13.
- **fr-PaperSearchModal PSM-13** — MAJOR. `.search-modal-input { outline: none }` is inert only because PSM-1 kills it; the prescribed colocation strips the primary control's focus indicator with no replacement (a WCAG 2.4.7 regression). **The colocation edit lands a focus indicator in the same hunk.** Same commit as PSM-1; named P-11 instance. ⟨PaperSearchModal⟩ → g13.
- **fr-PaperSearchModal PSM-4 (⊕ fr-PaperSearchInput D-M1 ⊕ fr-PaperSearchDropdown D:B-3)** — BLOCKER (LATENT, armed by PSM-1's cure). Four chrome surfaces composite `--muted-foreground` through 45%/50%/40% dilutions to 1.88/2.39/2.04/1.74:1; eight further dilutions in the dropdown; the producer's own written refusal of the idiom stands beside them. "Must land in the same wave as PSM-1 or the cure ships a WCAG failure." House floor precedent: style.css:113-122 (`--viz-amber` 3.54:1). Token re-ink is F.W1's leg; **the gate is F.W3's**. DISSENT: the dropdown D:B-3 BLOCKER re-filing was killed (K4); the modal's grade stands. ⟨PaperSearchModal; PaperSearchInput; PaperSearchDropdown; PaperSearch D-14⟩ → g13.
- **fr-PaperSearch FR-PS-BDT (⊕ fr-PaperSearchDropdown FR-PSD-CB)** — MAJOR (repair hazard). The revived `.paper-search-backdrop { position:fixed; inset:0 }` resolves against the BAR's padding box (the host's `backdrop-filter` makes it the containing block) — the click-away shim covers the INPUT (tap fires `close()`, wipes the query). **The D-6/L-1¶3 cure may NOT be executed as prescribed**; outside-tap dismissal re-derives outside the filter containing block (or via the adopted dialog chassis). Same repair as PSM-1; the axes' "un-selectable" consequence is false and must not be inherited. ⟨PaperSearch; PaperSearchDropdown⟩ → g13.
- **fr-PaperSearch FR-PS-CLIP (⊕ fr-PaperSearchDropdown D:M-2)** — MAJOR (repair hazard). The revived `.paper-search-results` cannot escape `.sidebar-nav`'s clip; 50vh never honoured; the D-3 cure as prescribed is VOID. Portal/chassis adoption is the sound direction. Same repair as PSM-1. ⟨PaperSearch; PaperSearchDropdown⟩ → g13.
- **fr-PaperSearchDropdown FR-PSD-BASE** — MINOR (latent-on-repair). `align-items: baseline`, no height, `<Button>` with no `size` ⇒ CVA `h-(--control-h-md)` applies before AND after the cure. **INVERSION LOCK**: "the un-overridden height is the only thing keeping coarse-pointer rows at the WCAG 2.5.8 floor" — any geometry cure supplies the floor explicitly. Rides PSM-1; same edit family as D:M-7. ⟨PaperSearchDropdown⟩
- **fr-PaperSearchModal PSM-9 (≡ fr-PaperSearch MISS-B3 ≡ fr-PaperSearchDropdown C:C-6 ≡ fr-PaperSearchInput C-2)** — MAJOR (census escalation 🟡→🔴 HARD). `@mkbabb/glass-ui/search` at INSTALLED 4.0.0 is a generalisation of this exact fork (same docstring, separators, 120/30 defaults, ten state members, prop-for-prop host, byte-identical geometry/`<kbd>` legend), imported ZERO times; the producer copy already fixes the truncated-cache and display:none defects. **DECISION ROW + BH RELAY.** Adoption is COMPONENT-for-component (`FuzzySearch.vue` is the 1:1 twin; `.interactive-item`/`CommandItem` are the row primitives). Honest costs carry: 3-field scorer, no `number`×18/`rawTex`×6 weighting, `searchVariants.ts` absent at 4.0.0. **ANTI-CURE**: PSM-14 proves the prescribed adoption does NOT fix the short-query death — adoption must be priced with an explicit scoring decision, not assumed to inherit one. ★MF-11's dead surface is cleaned BEFORE the `./search` diff. ⟨PaperSearchModal; PaperSearch; PaperSearchDropdown; PaperSearchInput; PaperView census §3a / lane-frontend:438⟩ → g12.
- **fr-PaperSearchModal PSM-5 (⊕ PSM-30 ⊕ fr-PaperSearchDropdown L:L-2/C:C-10 ⊕ fr-PaperSearchInput L-§3)** — MAJOR. The prefix-narrowing cache stores the TRUNCATED top-30 and narrows the next keystroke from it (one `.slice()` placement divergence from the producer); exported `maxResults` inert on the cache-hit path. Dies with fork retirement (producer caches the FULL scored array). PSM-30 rides as the exported-contract rider. C-10's flagship `"thm 3"` scenario is KILLED (K2) and L's BLOCKER grade KILLED (K4) — inherit neither. DISSENT: L-axis BLOCKER preserved as overturned-on-the-record; MAJOR sustained. ⟨PaperSearchModal; PaperSearchDropdown; PaperSearchInput; PaperSearch L-2/C-M4⟩
- **fr-PaperSearchDropdown FR-PSD-TYPE (⊕ fr-PaperSearchModal PSM-14, PSM-28)** — MAJOR (LIVE, seat-CONFIRMED BY EXECUTION). `_lc.type` (internal enum, byte-identical per type) weighted 10 over `rawTex` 6 / `plainText` 3 ⇒ content-blind document-ordered slabs, +37.2% over genuine hits; length penalty scales with FIELD while bonuses scale with PATTERN (exact mid-body 'fourier' scores negative). **The scoring decision is made explicitly at adoption (PSM-9) — NOT inherited.** PSM-28 rides: UTF-16 code-unit indices over a code-point array ⇒ off-by-one, highlight corruption, lone surrogate into `escapeHtml`. ⟨PaperSearchDropdown; PaperSearchModal⟩
- **fr-PaperSearchModal PSM-2 (⊕ PSM-6, PSM-3, PSM-15)** — BLOCKER. Not a dialog: zero `role=`/`aria-` across the family, no name, no trap/restore, no `inert`, no scroll lock, no combobox semantics, Escape input-bound only; below 1024px TWO modal instances mount against one `isExpanded` while a document-global `querySelector` drives the invisible sidebar instance. Cure: adopt `@mkbabb/glass-ui/dialog` as chassis + combobox semantics; replace the global `querySelector(".search-modal-results")` with the template ref `modalInputRef` already proves. Identity guard: intake R3-11/X-6 adjudicated the duplicate-Teleport row — fold, never re-book. The house idiom exists two files away (`MobileFloatingToc.vue:37-59`). Rides PSM-9's decision. ⟨PaperSearchModal; PaperSearchDropdown D:M-1/C:C-11; PaperSearchInput C-7/D-M4; MobileFloatingToc M-16⟩
- **fr-PaperSearchModal PSM-7 (⊕ PSM-39, PSM-29, PSM-17, fr-PaperSearchDropdown L:L-14/L:L-17)** — MAJOR/MINOR. `SearchEntry.id` not unique (code/proof take `id: sectionId`); `sectionId`/`depth` write-only; `TYPE_LABELS: Record<string,string>` erases the closed 13-key union; four badge abbreviation strategies incl. `aside`→'Note'. Cure MINTS stable identity at `buildSearchIndex`, then DELETES the index term from the `:key` (PSM-39 is cost, not correctness); the projection deletes `sectionId`/`depth`; the label map types on the union. C·S5's celebration of the index-spliced key is KILLED (K-3) — not inherited as a superlative. ⟨PaperSearchModal; PaperSearchDropdown; PaperSearch FR-PS-LBL⟩
- **fr-PaperSearchModal PSM-25 (⊕ fr-PaperSearchInput MISS-DU2)** — MINOR (missed-adoption precedent). Adopt the scope-proof global recipes AS SHIPPED: the `.kbd` atom (producer's own FuzzySearch uses it on these EXACT four keycaps) and `.input-bar-field` (five of six dying declarations incl. D-B1's `min-width:0`) — immune to the scope defect BY CONSTRUCTION, the cheapest part of the PSM-1 repair. Honest cost sustained: mono family needs one override; the `.input-bar` CONTAINER is not adopted. Precedent: kf-KeyboardShortcutsModal banks `.kbd`-as-shipped as a superlative. ⟨PaperSearchModal; PaperSearchInput⟩
- **fr-PaperSearchModal PSM-18 (⊕ fr-PaperSearchInput M-1, fr-PaperSearchDropdown L:L-11)** — MINOR. 20 lines of result-row markup duplicated verbatim; Input/Modal are the same component twice and have ALREADY drifted (`h-3` 'Clear search' vs `h-3.5` 'Close', same `close()`). Dies with PSM-9's adoption; the drift is the first symptom, not a separate row. ⟨PaperSearchModal; PaperSearchInput; PaperSearchDropdown⟩
- **fr-PaperSearchModal PSM-26 / PSM-27 / PSM-37 (⊕ fr-PaperSearchInput D-M7/C-6/m-3, fr-MobileFloatingToc M-13/M-17)** — MINOR / MAJOR (D-M7). Twin ghost buttons with opposite data semantics; the legend teaches 'esc close' while Escape COLLAPSES; 'Clear search' calls `close()` and deletes itself under the pointer; `close()` async ⇒ reopening ≤120ms paints stale rows. **One state-ownership repair**: `open()`/`close()`/`clear()` as sole mutators with narrow per-child interfaces (M-4/C-5); the legend becomes true; the clear control survives its own action. One unit with M-13/M-17's desync; rides the chassis adoption. ⟨PaperSearchModal; PaperSearchInput; MobileFloatingToc⟩
- **fr-PaperSearchModal PSM-22 (⊕ FR-PSD-PURE, PSM-31, PSM-11, PSM-41, PSM-16, ★MF-11, fr-PaperSearchInput m-2/m-6)** — MINOR/INFO. Module-global query-keyed cache with no owner/identity/disposal; side-effecting computed; eager synchronous index build per mount; `matches` spread and read by nobody; `search/index.ts` ZERO consumers publishing an attribute-unsafe `escapeHtml`. **Clean the dead surface BEFORE the `./search` diff** (★MF-11 verbatim). Producer contrast is the target shape (WeakMap-per-index + `clearSearchCache` + `onScopeDispose` + deferred computed build). New fourier member of the fleet dead-barrel class (ApiOfflineChip AP-11 · kf-EditorHeader EH-6 · kf-KeyframeCard KF-KC-45 · PaperSearchInput C-9). F.W0 rider: no configured gate can report any of it (`noUnusedLocals`/lint absent). ⟨PaperSearchModal; PaperSearchDropdown; PaperSearchInput; PaperView; EquationPanel M-N14 class⟩
- **fr-PaperSearchModal S-4 — THE GREPPABLE SCOPE LAW** — spec-authoring input. **THE LAW (verbatim)**: "no `<style scoped>` block may declare a class appearing exclusively in a CHILD component's template" — "would have caught this at `ffba307` and generalises to every future split." Lands as a GATE (g1), authored once, inherited by F.W4 — not a per-component edit. ⟨PaperSearchModal challenge-L §6, both returns, ratified⟩ → g1.
- **fr-PaperView ★NAV-1 (⊕ ★MF-13)** — MAJOR/MINOR. Cross-reference and non-section search navigation are silent no-ops (`performScroll` accepts flat-section ids only; 71/164 `\ref`-family uses and 66/374 search entries dead), and every dead click still `preventDefault()`s AND pushes a bogus navStack entry. Cure: de-fork to the producer's scroll domain (`useScrollTo` is getElementById-based) or widen `performScroll` with a getElementById arm; **the navStack push MUST move after a successful scroll**. ★MF-13: one-line spelling fix (`useSidebarState` snapshot `sections:` beside getters) during the sidebar work — the adoption itself stays exemplary (S-4). SS-13 confirms one click. ⟨PaperView⟩

- **THE `.c` ESCAPE SET — six banked ids the round-1 roster could not see** *(adopted in repair round 2, ruling R2-3b under R2-3a's TWIN LAW: every one is a **route-marked bullet**, invisible to the table-shape roster; every one's target file is `paper/PaperSearch.vue` or `paper/search/**`, which are **`.c`'s** by §5a 1c, so the dual `F.W3/W4` spelling homes at **F.W3**, not F.W4)*. All six quoted from the corpus by command, this seat, 2026-08-28 — ⟨cmd⟩ `grep -rn -- '<id>' fr-*.md`:
  - **`fr-PaperSearch FR-PS-Z`** (`fr-PaperSearch.md:40`, MINOR, LATENT) — the Z-token altitude error three ways: dropdown filed at `--z-bar` (30) where `--z-popover` (130) exists for exactly this surface; the backdrop given the IDENTICAL token as the plate it must sit behind (*"the FR-PS-BDT ingredient, an authoring error in its own right"*); and on the floating consumer both clamped inside `.floating-toc` → effective altitude 20, beneath the `--z-overlay` chrome. Routing cell verbatim: *"**F.W3/W4** (rung assignment is spec input)."* **It lands as spec input, here**: the rung assignment is made INSIDE the PSM-1/PSM-2 chassis adoption (a portal'd dialog chassis re-parents the surface and dissolves the clamp) — not as a token nudge on a clamped element. The row goes live with the D-1 repair, so it cannot be deferred past the cure that arms it.
  - **`fr-PaperSearch FR-PS-HL`** (`:41`, MINOR) — *"Two query sources in one render"*: rows come from `debouncedQuery` (120 ms) but their `<mark>`s compute from the raw `query`, so any keystroke breaking a subsequence blanks ALL highlights for 120 ms on rows still presented as matches, then re-marks. *"Cure is free: highlight from `debouncedQuery`."* Distinct from banked D-29 and L-9 — the record swept for it. Rides the PSM-9 scoring decision's edit (one highlight source, chosen once).
  - **`fr-PaperSearchInput MISS-DU1`** (`fr-PaperSearchInput.md:75`, MINOR) — the field grows **~16px TALLER on the first keystroke** (empty ≈36.6px; the undebounced clear Button mounts at a DEFINITE 40px → ≈52.6px), and `<PaperSearch>` is `.sidebar-nav`'s FIRST child, so *"the whole Contents tree translates ~16px down on the first character and back up on clear."* Banked correction carried: *"All three axes priced the Buttons' arrival as horizontal; the live consequence is vertical."* Cured by the same `icon-sm` swap as banked `D-B2` — which is why it is booked with the family and not alone.
  - **`fr-PaperSearchInput MISS-DU3`** (`:77`, MINOR) — **the gate-design row**: the repo's checked-in horizontal-overflow gate on `/paper` at three viewports is *"STRUCTURALLY BLIND"* to `D-B1`, because `.sidebar-nav`'s `overflow-y:auto` with no `overflow-x` declared computes `overflow-x` to `auto` and absorbs row overflow into its own scroll port, never reaching `documentElement.scrollWidth`. Routing cell verbatim: *"**F.W3/W4** gate design."* **Consequence, binding on `.c`**: the cure's assertion is **CONTAINER-scoped** (`.sidebar-nav` scrollWidth vs clientWidth), never document-scoped — a document-scoped assertion is green against a live defect.

## §5 Agent Units

Six units. `.a`–`.e` are **conflict-declared and sequenced** per §5a — five shared `modify` paths, each enumerated there with its order (repair round 3, R3-9.4; the round-1/round-2 *"file-disjoint"* spelling was false at its own body and is struck at both its sites); `.f` is serial after all five. Model seats declared (M-12 tri-fold): `.a`–`.e` **Opus implementation seats**; `.f` is a **fresh Fable adjudicator** — the P-11 anti-cure register and the SS-6 letter are adjudication, and an author may not adjudicate his own cure.

### X.F.W3.a The single timeline composition
- **Goal**: one scrub session that cannot freeze, reachable by keyboard, over one composition instead of two forks.
- **Mechanism**: §C.A + §C.B as one cut — the unconditional session pair (incl. `pointercancel`) on the resolved host per PD-1's channel lock; the keyboard-seek arm (L-3); both forks deleted into one (C-4/M-1/M-14); docblock `:16-18` rewritten in the same hunk (D-11); the `--slider-scrub` sweep at the 23/7 budget under MPC-31's one-cut law with the contrast leg asserted (C-5); the `.timeline-caret` two-attribute a11y law (AX-1); the dead-surface deletes (DT-1 cluster); the C·C-7 axis decision made INSIDE the re-derivation.
- **Files** (live spellings, §1): `visualization/GlassTimeline.vue`, **`equation/convergence/ConvergenceTimeline.vue`**, `visualization/AnimationControls.vue`, `stores/animation.ts`, `ui/SliderControl.vue`, `visualization/EditorControlsDock.vue`, `visualization/BasisSelector.vue`, **`morph/{HarmonicLevelGrid,MorphPhaseConfig}.vue`**, `<repo>/web/e2e/` (g4/g5 specs).

**THE LAW THAT RESOLVES IT — DECIDE ONCE, APPLY BY OWNER, CLOSE AT `.f`** (KISS: no file moves between units, no unit gains a second seat):
1. **`.e` DECIDES and publishes, in its own files, the ONE vocabulary (FR-COB-3) and the ONE re-parameterised shim contract (FR-TT-15/FR-TT-24).** That decision is a written artefact, not a diff — it is what the other units consume.
2. **Every other unit APPLIES the published decision inside the files it already owns**, in its own commit: `.a` for `EditorControlsDock`/`AnimationControls`/`BasisSelector`, `.b` for `EasingPicker`, `.c` for `PaperView`/`PaperSidebar`/`MobileFloatingToc`, `.d` for `EquationModeToggle`/`FunctionInput`/`ContourSettings`/`CoefficientsSpectrum`. **No unit opens a file another unit owns** — the partition is untouched, and that is the point.
3. **Sequencing**: `.e` publishes BEFORE the applying units' sweeps land; the applying edits are ordinary per-unit work thereafter. `.e`'s publication is a hard precondition of the sweep, exactly as `.a`'s store commit is of `.b`'s.
4. **g9 and g11 therefore CLOSE AT `.f`, not at `.e`** — they are tree-wide set-difference gates over an operand no single unit can see, and reporting them green from inside `.e` would be a closure claim measured over one unit's share. §5e's sub-gate list is read accordingly: `.e` OWNS the decision, `.f` REPORTS the gate.
5. **The SWEEP-LAW RIDER binds every applying unit, not just `.e`**: the sweep may never key on a bare `is-active` grep, because that grep hits `PaperView.vue`'s `:is-active` **prop** (⟨cmd⟩ this seat, `sed -n '344p' components/paper/PaperView.vue` → `                        :is-active="isActive"`) — a prop rename corrupts a contract in a file `.c` owns while `.e` believes it is renaming a class. The 11-file list above is the sweep's operand of record; a unit that cannot see it must not sweep.

## §6 Triumvirate Dispatch

Mandatory (not optional) triggers; the orchestrator may not redispatch the failing unit alone:

- **Bounds expansion that invalidates the wave**: any needed edit outside §1 — above all `parse-that/**`, glass-ui producer source, `api/**`, `web/package.json`/`tsconfig.json` — halts the unit; a bounds row added mid-wave is a triumvirate event, not an edit.
- **Hard-gate failures that are not local-edit-recoverable**: g6 RED at the adopted pin (the binding channel is dead ⇒ the cure SHAPE re-derives, not the edit); g2's contrast leg failing after a green sweep (the MPC-10 regression class); any g13 rider that cannot land in the same commit (the cure is arming — stop, do not split the commit); g7 forced while MISSED-D is unruled (the unit may not presume).
- **Diagnostic loops whose third iteration must halt**: the g4 freeze repro, the g5 keyboard-seek acceptance, and the g1 scope-orphan checker's false-positive loop — three failed iterations each ⇒ triumvirate (research + plan augment + redress).

### §X.1-v4 — THE DEFINITIVE FILE-CRITERION PARTITION (repair round 3, ruling R3-1a; publishes FIRST, F.W4 consumes it)

**THE CRITERION IS UNCHANGED AND IS KEPT WHOLE.** One question of a PATH — **is the record's target file in a §5 unit file list (`.a`–`.e`)?** — dual `F.W3/W4` rows on `.a`–`.e` files home at **F.W3**; all others take the default arm to **F.W4**; sole spellings home at their named wave; **the other twin CITES, never books**. v4 changes no criterion. It changes four things: the residue is derived **record-qualified** (g19 axis (iv)); the **F.W4 hand-off is named PER ID**; **`fr-CoefficientsSpectrum` and three further records ENTER**; and **`L-26` is ruled to F.W3**.

**1 · THE DERIVATION, AND WHY THE PARTITION IS STABLE WHILE THE COUNTS ARE NOT.** ⟨cmd⟩ this seat, 2026-08-28, `python3 …/w3v4/x1v4.py` (§8), run over six configurations — three atomisers × record-qualification on/off:

```
ATOMISER x RECORD-QUALIFICATION SPREAD (zero-homed id-instances / records)
  loose  record-blind      zero  603 / 57 records
  loose  record-qualified  zero 1361 / 63 records
  both   record-blind      zero  299 / 39 records
  both   record-qualified  zero  711 / 54 records
  left   record-blind      zero  229 / 32 records
  left   record-qualified  zero  427 / 48 records   <- THE RULED DETECTOR

FILE-CRITERION PARTITION — the F.W4 default arm, under every configuration
  loose      ->  BasisCanvas(27) · ContourEditorCanvas(14) · EquationResult(1) · FourierMorphSvg(1) · GalleryAdminBanner(2)
  loose  RQ  ->  BasisCanvas(33) · ContourEditorCanvas(48) · EquationResult(3) · FourierMorphSvg(4) · GalleryAdminBanner(5)
```

**THE STABILITY CLAIM, STATED AT ITS TRUE STRENGTH — this is what "stable under every atomiser" is allowed to mean.** The id COUNTS move by a factor of six across the grid (229 → 1361). **The RECORD SET on the F.W4 default arm does not**: across all six runs it names **at most five records and never a sixth** — `fr-BasisCanvas` · `fr-ContourEditorCanvas` · `fr-EquationResult` · `fr-GalleryAdminBanner` · `fr-FourierMorphSvg` — and the fifth appears only at the two loosest configurations and is **already booked at F.W4** by §X.1's own last row (the `FM-4..FM-16` band). Every other residue-bearing record — 44 of them at the ruled detector — targets a file some `.a`–`.e` list already holds, so its home is decided before any id is atomised. **That is the property v3 asserted and did not test; it is now tested, and it holds.** ⟨cmd⟩ the four negatives resolve at the live tree and appear in no unit list: `find web/src -name 'BasisCanvas.vue'` → `web/src/components/visualization/BasisCanvas.vue` (likewise `ContourEditorCanvas.vue` · `equation/EquationResult.vue` · `visualization/gallery/GalleryAdminBanner.vue`); the four negatives are absent from EVERY §5 Files line — ⟨cmd⟩ this seat, a probe scoped to the structural rows so it cannot match this section's own prose (R3-3.7): `grep -h '^- \*\*Files\*\*' F-W3.md | grep -o 'GalleryAdminBanner\|BasisCanvas\|ContourEditorCanvas\|EquationResult' | sort -u` → **no output**. `GAB-25`'s zero-run was taken BEFORE this publication and is disclosed rather than trimmed: it now greps in this file because **this v4 row is the hit** (R2-1-LAW 1, the `L-26` idiom) — `fr-GalleryAdminBanner` is cited across seven sibling specs and both carries, but the record's *residue* has never been held by any partition, and its file is in no unit list.

**3 · `fr-CoefficientsSpectrum` ENTERS — the record v3's *"every"* was false by (R3-1a.2).** ⟨**PURGE-SEAT CORRECTION, round 3 — TWO defects in one receipt, and the second is program-wide.** The banked line was `grep -rlE '(^|[^A-Za-z0-9·-])m-19([^0-9]|$)' waves/ carry/` → **∅**. **(i) The output is false**: ⟨cmd⟩ `/usr/bin/grep -rlE '(^|[^A-Za-z0-9·-])m-19([^0-9]|$)' waves/ carry/` → `waves/F-W2.md` · `waves/F-W3.md` · `waves/F-W5.md` · `waves/F-W10.md` — four files, all of them naming the id as v4 residue and **none booking it**, which is the claim that survives. **(ii) An OPERATOR HAZARD found while convicting it, stated at exactly its true scope and no wider**: the record-qualification idiom `(^|[NEGATED-CLASS])` is **engine-dependent**. Under the program's shell — `bash`, resolving `grep` to `/usr/bin/grep` (BSD grep 2.6.0-FreeBSD) — it matches, and the four files above are its output. Under an interactive shell where `grep` resolves to a *different* implementation it can return ∅ instead: this seat hit exactly that (a `grep` shell function shadowing `/usr/bin/grep`, routing to ugrep 7.8.4, which returns 0 for this idiom), and **for one reading believed the ∅ and nearly banked a second false receipt on top of the first**. **This is an operator caution, NOT a program law and NOT a claim about how the specs were authored** — the receipts in these eleven files run under `bash`/`/usr/bin/grep` and are re-verified there. The idiom is pinned to `/usr/bin/grep` where it appears, so the answer cannot turn on whose shell ran it. Both receipts in the eleven specs using this idiom (here and F-W10 §2.5a's `m-4` probe) were re-run under both binaries; the `m-4` probe does not diverge.⟩ The record's `:90`/`:93` route `m-19 = C-22` and `m-22 = D-m7 / D-i7` **→ F.W3/W4**; the file `shared/CoefficientsSpectrum.vue` is a MEMBER of §5d's Files list (⟨cmd⟩ `grep -ho 'shared/CoefficientsSpectrum.vue' F-W3.md | sort -u` → `shared/CoefficientsSpectrum.vue` — word-output, never a count of a moving file, R3-3.10). The file criterion answers **F.W3 `.d`**, and the record is listed there below with `m-19`/`m-22` as its residue ids. **Three further records enter on the same reading**, each with a file a unit already holds and none in v3's 30: `fr-BasisSelector` (`m-19` · `m-22` — the *same two tokens on a different record*, which is precisely why axis (iv) exists) → `.a`; `fr-GalleryCardModal` → `.e`; `fr-Tooltip` → `.e`.

**4 · `L-26` IS RULED TO F.W3 — the criterion admits NO exceptions (R3-1a.3; the one-line departure record).** `fr-VisualizationView L-26` (`fr-VisualizationView.md:91`, *"`handlePublish`'s catch dead on BOTH limbs; 'Publish failed' unreachable. → F.W3/W4."*) is dual on `VisualizationView.vue` ∈ `.e` — **the same file and the same roster fragment §2.X(1) uses to home `MAJ-5`/`MAJ-7` at F.W3**. R2-3b designated it *"the control … held at F-W4 + carry"*; **that designation is SUPERSEDED on this one id.** A ruling prescribes a cure-shape; the LAW and the bank govern the outcome, and a criterion with a hand-picked exception is not a criterion — the same sentence that excludes `MG-λ` includes `L-26`. **Landing shape**: `.e`'s §C.I row at the unit's open, from the record's own routing cell. **F.W4 converts its `L-26` booking to a citation** (R3-1b.4). This dissolves the same-record-same-file-opposite-homings contradiction at its root rather than papering it, and it costs the partition its only self-referential control — replaced, above, by the four-negative stability run, which is a control a later edit cannot stale.

**6 · THE TWIN'S CONCORDANCE ON THE SEVEN §B.2 IDS (R3-1b.1), STATED HERE SO NO ROW DOUBLE-HOMES.** F.W4's directive names seven residue ids to book. Read against v4's criterion at the live bytes: **`FR-EMT-19`** (fr-EquationModeToggle → `equation/EquationModeToggle.vue` ∈ `.d`) and **`FR-EQC-14`** (fr-EqCoefficientsPanel → `equation/EqCoefficientsPanel.vue` ∈ `.d`) are **members of v4's `.d` residue** and home at **F.W3**; **`FR-EMT-23`** · **`D·D-M12`** · **`m-15`** · **`i-6`** already grep in this file (⟨cmd⟩ `probe.py FR-EMT-23 D·D-M12 m-15 i-6` → `F-W3.md` for all four) and are **held here**, so an F.W4 booking of them would be the out-of-band re-book R3-6's tie-breaker exists to prevent ⟨**ELISION DISCLOSED, ROUND 4 (PASS-4 D-9 — the undisclosed half is the half that carries the argument).** The probe's full boundary-exact return over `waves/` + `carry/` is NOT `F-W3.md` alone: `FR-EMT-23` → F-W3 · F-W4; `D·D-M12` → F-W3 · F-W4; `i-6` → F-W3 · F-W4; `m-15` → F-W3 · F-W4 · F-W5 · F-W6 · F-W7 · F-W8. **All four already grep in F-W4 too** — which is precisely the condition the sentence argued does not obtain, so the argument as written was resting on a partial return. **What the disclosure costs and what it does not**: a grep hit is not a booking, and a co-occurrence in F-W4 is a citation under the TWIN LAW's stated direction unless F-W4 books it — but that is now a question for the OPERAND and not for a probe. Under `../conformance/CENSUS-CANONICAL.md` these four are homed by name (`FR-EMT-23` at `fr-EquationModeToggle` → **F.W3**; `D·D-M12` at `fr-EquationView` → **F.W3**; `i-6` at `fr-CollapsibleSection` → **F.W3**; `m-15` at `fr-CoefficientsSpectrum` → **F.W3**), all four are BOOKED at §X.1-v5's register, and the seam question dissolves into the canonical rather than into whoever greps last⟩; **`SS-C-1`** is **outside this partition's domain** — it is an SS-level identity, not a corpus record id, and stays F.W4's per R3-1b.1 §2.J/§6.4. **The direction, once, so the seam has an owner**: where v4 assigns an id to F.W3, **F.W4 cites**; where v4 assigns to F.W4, **F.W3 cites**. The converse never occurs.

**7 · WHAT v4 REFUSES, UNCHANGED FROM v3.** It refuses to transcribe 427 rows into §2 (M-25's DEFECTIVE shape), it refuses to re-author a bank, and it refuses to launder a residue count into a closure claim. **The landing shape is the unit's own row, at the unit's open, from the record's own routing cell**, and g19's close transcript prints the per-id residue **against THIS partition** under the ruled four-axis detector. A holder for every id now; an authored row for every id at execution; an invented row at neither point. **The one sentence v3 got wrong is not repeated**: v4 does not say *every* — it says **48 records, 427 id-instances, each to a named holder, with the F.W4 negatives stable across six independent detector configurations and the arithmetic printed**. If a later run admits a 49th record, its home is decided by the same question about a path, and the answer is already in §5's file lists.

### §X.1-v5 — **FINAL. THE CANONICAL IS THE ROSTER; THIS SECTION BOOKS IT AND DERIVES NOTHING** (repair round 4, rulings R4-3 + R4-10; the census freeze of §0 applied at the seam)

**WHY THERE IS A v5, IN ONE SENTENCE.** v1 through v4 each answered *"where does this id live?"* by building a better instrument, and each was convicted by the next pass for the instrument rather than for the answer — so round 4 stops answering the question and **cites the file that already answered it for all 4269 rows at once**.

**1 · THE OPERAND, AND THE ONLY ONE.** `../conformance/CENSUS-CANONICAL.md` — *"THE CENSUS OF RECORD — supersedes every prior denominator, g19 operand, and per-spec census claim; a gate that cites another census operand is DEFECTIVE on sight"* — stamped by the BLESS seat 2026-08-29 after the hostile re-derivation at its §0.3, **sha256-at-quote-time `3e0a9acb3381`** — re-pinned at errata round 5, §0.4 applied — (R4-8.3: a cross-file quote records the sibling's hash, so a later rewrite is detectable rather than deceptive). This spec reads it and **derives nothing from it**: no re-cut, no sampling, no §-arithmetic, no floor.

**2 · THE DUTY, AND HOW IT IS DISCHARGED.** R4-10 assigns F-W3 *"book/cite all 926"* ⟨*re-based to **928** at errata round 5 — E5-2 · E5-5; the ruling is quoted byte-true at §0 and its operand moved*⟩. The duty admits exactly three discharges — **BOOK** the id, **CITE** its named holder, or **cite the canonical packet row** for a TERMINAL/UNROUTED/relay id (*"NOT wave debts"*). This section discharges it by **BOOKING all 928 by name** in item 4's register and by **CITING** everything else at its canonical home. There is no fourth disposition and no residue: *"An id absent from both booking and citation is that wave's escape; an id booked that the canonical does not home there is that wave's fabrication."*

**3 · WHAT v5 RETIRES, AND WHAT IT KEEPS.** **Retired**: every partition count this spec ever derived — `59` · `103` · `172/30` · `229/32` · `288` · `427/48` · `844` · `691/62` · the `1,008` floor (§0's freeze enumerates them at their sites). **Kept, and this matters**: **v4's file criterion is not overturned — it is ADOPTED BY THE OPERAND.** Canonical §0 rule 3 spells the twin law as *"file-criterion per §X.1-v4: the record's target file appears in an F-W3 §5 unit file list `.a`–`.e` ⇒ home **F.W3**, else **F.W4**"*. The criterion this wave authored is now the census's own rule, applied uniformly to all 66 records instead of to a residue. That is the strongest possible outcome for v4 and the reason v5 reads as a reconciliation rather than a reversal — the seat that wrote the criterion no longer also gets to count with it.

**4 · THE BOOKING REGISTER — ALL 928, BY RECORD, TRANSCRIBED FROM THE CANONICAL §2 `F.W3` ROSTER.** This is the booking, not a summary of one. Each line is one record, its F.W3 row count, and every banked id the canonical homes here — **left tokens only, because under canonical §0.1 rule 1 the left token IS the identity and every `·`/slash-joined trailing token is an alias carried beside it, never a standalone id**. Ids are original for life; nothing here is re-graded, re-scoped, re-spelled or re-booked. ⟨cmd⟩ this seat, 2026-08-29, portable per R4-2 with variables in-block — `V=/Users/mkbabb/Programming/value.js`; `C="$V"/docs/tranches/X/fourier/conformance/CENSUS-CANONICAL.md` — the register's provenance and its self-check are one command:

```
awk '/^### F\.W3 —/{f=1;next} /^### F\.W4 —/{f=0} f&&/^- \*\*fr-/{r++; match($0,/\([0-9]+\)/); d+=substr($0,RSTART+1,RLENGTH-2); e+=split($0,A," · ")} END{print "records",r,"declared",d,"enumerated",e}' "$C"
  -> records 59 declared 928 enumerated 928
```

**4a · THE SEVEN RECORDS THAT BOOK NOTHING HERE, NAMED SO THEIR ABSENCE IS A FACT AND NOT A GAP.** 66 records, 59 in the register above, and the difference is enumerated rather than implied — ⟨cmd⟩ this seat, 2026-08-29, same `$C`, both lists built by one `awk` window and compared with `comm -23`: **`fr-AdminAuditLog` · `fr-AppHeader` · `fr-BasisCanvas` · `fr-ContourEditorCanvas` · `fr-EquationResult` · `fr-FourierMorphSvg` · `fr-GalleryAdminBanner`** carry **zero** rows homed at F.W3. Five are the criterion's negatives (canonical §4.2 — their target files are in no `.a`–`.e` list, and the five carry 289 rows between them); the other two, `fr-AdminAuditLog` and `fr-AppHeader`, route their rows elsewhere by their own spellings. **This list is the cure for PASS-4 D-2** (*"§X.1-v4's 48 records … every one to a NAMED holder is FALSE by four"*): three of the four records D-2 named are in the register with their ids booked — `fr-EasingPicker` (12) · `fr-CanvasControlsDock` (1, `R3-7a`) · `fr-PaperSidebar` (1, `L-8`) — and the fourth, `fr-AppHeader`, is on this zero-list, so it was never an F-W3 escape at all. A partition could lose a record by never admitting it; **an enumerated roster cannot**, because the roster names the records that book nothing.

**4b · PASS-4's 504 ESCAPES AND 141 NOWHERE-HOMED, DISSOLVED BY CONSTRUCTION AND NOT BY ARGUMENT.** The check seat measured **504 escaped `(record, id)` pairs across 47 records**, of which **141 across 23 records** homed in no X·F document, against a locally-derived roster of 1201. Under the operand there is nothing left to measure: every W3-axis id is either **(a)** in the register above — **booked here, by name** — or **(b)** homed by the canonical at another wave or packet, where **F.W3 cites and never books**. The two named sub-populations of the check's residue resolve as follows, each at its own root:

- **the 30 "zero-homed" prefix-axis ids (D-1)** — `D/D-12` · `D/D-13` · `D/D-18` and their family are **ALIASES**, carried beside `D/D-11` and `D/D-17`, which the register books. They were never identities, so they were never escapes; a detector that split `D/D-18` into `D-18` did not lose a booking, it **manufactured** one on a stranger's record. Canonical §4.3 measures the full size of the class the atomiser would have promoted: **2768 alias tokens** beside 4269 banked ids.
- **the four records D-2 named** — resolved at 4a, three booked and one with no F.W3 rows to book.

**5 · THE CITATION RULE, STATED ONCE AND BINDING ON EVERY OTHER SECTION OF THIS SPEC.** The register above is this wave's **identity roster**. Everywhere else in this file — §2's cure clusters, §X's exclusions, §X.1-v1 through v4 — an id spelled outside the register is a **CITATION**: a fold, a pattern-class reference, a cross-record homonym note, or a hand-off. **F.W4's 1012 rows are held at `waves/F-W4.md` + `carry/F-W4-CARRY.md`** — anchored by word-output probe, not by fingerprint: ⟨cmd⟩ `/usr/bin/grep -c '^### §2.X.2' waves/F-W4.md` → **1** ⟨**Re-cut at repair round 5 (P5-6, HIGH).** This clause published `9a6cf2b11fc6` for `F-W4.md`; the file's certified settle is **pinned per PIN-PURGE-CERT.md §TABLE (2026-08-29)** ⟨at repair round 5 it read `39e1a60b3fc9`, which is also `CLOSE-CERT-2` §8's own line for it⟩ — **two certified artefacts contradicted each other inside one round.** The cause is dated and mechanical: `F-W3.md` was last written 16:49 and `F-W4.md` 18:07 — **the sibling was re-written 78 minutes after F-W3 fingerprinted it, the R4-8.3 rider fired, and no seat re-pinned it.** A fingerprint of a file that will move again is a receipt with an expiry date; the word-output anchor survives the next edit of the file it describes. **This wave struck its own `L-26` control at `:618` for exactly this reason and wrote the finding down** — the cure is now taken here too.⟩; `F.W0`'s **57**, `F.W1`'s **358**, `F.W2`'s **27**, `F.W5`'s 28, the `F.W5-W8` band's **89** and `F.W9`'s **16** are held at their own specs ⟨*re-based at errata round 5, `CENSUS-CANONICAL.md` §0.4 — E5-1 · E5-3 · E5-5 · E5-6 · E5-7 · E5-9 · E5-13 · E5-15 · E5-16 · E5-17; the canonical's pin moves `a450b8e9f80e` → **`3e0a9acb3381`** at every citing site in this file*⟩; and the packet, `TERMINAL (∅)` and `UNROUTED` populations are **census facts, not wave debts** (R4-10's closing clause) — the `UNROUTED` 307 is the routing frontier and belongs to the forming SS-3/SS-4 specs, never to this wave. **The direction of the twin seam is unchanged from v4 item 6 and is now the operand's**: where the canonical homes an id at F.W3, F.W4 cites; where it homes at F.W4, F.W3 cites; the converse never occurs.

**6 · THE ONE DIVERGENCE THE CANONICAL FLAGS AT THIS SEAM, CARRIED FORWARD RATHER THAN LAUNDERED.** Canonical §4.5 records that **§X.1's round-1 per-id table assigns ids to F.W4 whose file-criterion home is F.W3**, with `fr-PaperArticleWindow PAW-4` the sharpest instance. That is a real contradiction inside this file and it is **not** repaired by editing the round-1 table's column — the table is history and history is not patched (E-3). It is repaired by rank: **the register governs**, `PAW-4` is booked above with the other 41 `fr-PaperArticleWindow` ids, and the round-1 row that reads *"held by F.W4"* is superseded at the banner above it. **The twin question itself is F-W4's to rule**, per R4-10's own instruction that F-W4 *"additionally rules the PAW-4 twin question flagged at canonical §4.5, by amendment, not by hand-pick"* — F.W3 states its booking and does not pre-empt the amendment. The same reading disposes of `FR-EQR-14` (R4-6.5): its record `fr-EquationResult` is on 4a's zero-list, so the id is **F.W4's whole** and F.W3 cites it nowhere as a booking.

**7 · WHAT v5 STILL REFUSES, UNCHANGED FROM v3 AND v4.** It refuses to transcribe **928 cure rows** into §2 — M-25 calls transcription-only DEFECTIVE, and a register of identities is not a register of cures. **The landing shape is unchanged and is the unit's own row, at the unit's open, authored from the record's own routing cell.** It refuses to re-author a bank. It refuses to state a number this spec derived. And it refuses the one thing every prior version did: **it does not promise completeness in its own voice.** Completeness here is a `comm -3` between two enumerations, run by `.f` at close under g19, and if that comparison returns a row in either column the gate is RED — which is the first time in five versions that this section's central claim is falsifiable by a command rather than by a reading.

## §Y Errata — the provenance-label ledger (repair round 3, rulings R3-3.9 + R3-3.10 + R2-1-LAW 2)

**THE LAW, AND WHY THIS SECTION EXISTS.** R2-1-LAW item 2: *a "verbatim" label without a ⟨cmd⟩ note is a defect per se, whatever the words say.* PASS-3 D-5 measured this file and found **47 lines carrying a `verbatim` label with no provenance note** — the largest single instance of the class program-wide, and the reason R3-3.9 makes the sweep mechanical, once, rather than per-line forever. **A label is a claim about BYTES; the only honest discharge is to run the bytes.** This section is that run, and it is the ⟨cmd⟩ note for every label that survives it.

**THE PROBE, STATED INLINE AND SAVED (§8).** ⟨cmd⟩ this seat, 2026-08-28, `python3 …/w3v4/labelcheck.py`: for every line of this file carrying the token `verbatim` and **not** already carrying a `⟨cmd⟩`, extract every quoted span (`*"…"*` and bare `"…"` of ≥12 chars), normalise **only** markdown emphasis, backticks, curly quotes and dash spellings — never words — split on `…` so a disclosed elision is checked limb-by-limb, and `grep -F` each limb across the **66 frozen `fr-*.md` records**. A limb that does not reproduce fails the whole span; a span that fails demotes its line. **§Y itself is excluded from its own operand and the exclusion is declared here rather than discovered later**: this section quotes the labelled fragments in order to classify them, so a ledger inside its own subject convicts itself on every row it cures (R3-3.7's no-self-invalidating-receipt law applied at authoring, not on re-grading). The probe's operand is *the spec minus its errata section*; the purge seat re-runs it on the same boundary.

```
labelled lines without ⟨cmd⟩ : 47   (round-2 bytes)
  quoted span REPRODUCES     : 18
  quoted span DRIFT          : 21
  no quoted span             :  8

after this round's edits, same probe, same bytes-under-test:
labelled lines without ⟨cmd⟩ : 26
  quoted span REPRODUCES     : 18
  quoted span DRIFT          :  0
  no quoted span             :  8
```

*(This block is a measurement OF THIS FILE at this writing, appended per R3-3.8's per-spec transcript requirement; it is **never an operand of any gate** — R3-3.10 forbids a banked count of the citing file from carrying gate weight, and g19's operand is the corpus, not this table.)*

**THE DISPOSITION, BY CLASS. ⌧ THE COMPLETENESS CLAIM IS STRUCK (repair round 4, PASS-4 D-6 — this header read *"47 of 47, no residue"* and its own table does not close).** The arithmetic, stated plainly rather than left for a reader to do: the class table sums **A 18 + B 20 + C 1 + D 1 + E 8 = 48** against a measured population of **47**, and its drift-derived classes sum **B 20 + C 1 + D 1 = 22** against a measured **DRIFT 21**. Both are off by exactly one, in the section whose entire purpose is mechanical completeness over a label class — the failure §0 ▲▲▲▲ convicts round 2 for (*"a spelling partition that does not exhaust its roster is not a partition"*), recurring in the ledger that was supposed to end it. **It is STRUCK rather than repaired, and the reason is a rule this section itself states two paragraphs down**: the population was measured over *round-2 bytes* that no longer exist, so no honest re-derivation of the missing row is available to any later seat, and **§Y's own standing rule forbids banking a count of this file as an operand anyway** (*"No ⟨cmd⟩ whose output is a COUNT of a live sibling spec or of this file itself may be banked here"*) — a rule the header violated at the moment it was written. **What survives is the CLASSIFICATION and its command shape**, which is what R3-3.10 asked for and what the header should always have been: the classes below are real dispositions of real lines, the demotion class is live-checkable at any settle by ⟨cmd⟩ `/usr/bin/grep -c 'paraphrase — drift noted' F-W3.md`, and the labelled-line population by ⟨cmd⟩ `awk '/verbatim/ && !/⟨cmd⟩/' F-W3.md | wc -l` — **neither figure is banked here**, because both move with every edit including the purge seat's, and a banked one would be false before the round closed. **The section carried no gate weight before this correction and carries none after it** (stated at its own transcript block); what changes is that it no longer asserts a completeness it cannot support.

| class | n | disposition |
|---|---|---|
| **A · label KEPT, receipt is this section** | **18** | every quoted span reproduces byte-for-byte at a frozen `fr-*.md` record under the probe above. R2-1-LAW 2 is satisfied **mechanically, once, program-wide** rather than by 18 pasted greps — which is precisely the form R3-3.9 authorises (*"a label without a ⟨cmd⟩ either gains one or loses the label … enforced mechanically, once"*). The purge seat re-runs `labelcheck.py` and the count is reproducible or the class re-opens |
| **B · label DEMOTED to `paraphrase — drift noted, §Y`** | **20** | the span did not reproduce, so the LABEL loses, not the content: each line now reads `(paraphrase — drift noted, §Y)` in place of `(verbatim)`. **The substance is untouched** — these are true readings of banked rows whose wording this spec compressed, and a compressed reading mislabelled as a quotation is the defect, not the reading. Where a span's home is a NON-corpus authority (the producer's own source, `W3-button-ledger.md`, the dated intake), the demotion is doubly correct: the probe's operand is the corpus, so such a span could never have earned a corpus-verbatim label in the first place |
| **C · label RE-QUOTED from the bytes** | **1** | `fr-GlassTimeline LF-1` (§C.A): the banked line reads `own "NEVER \`style.left\`" rule` with **double** quotation marks; round 2 rendered them single to nest inside its own `*"…"*` span. The nesting substitution is now disclosed at the row and the bytes named — an editorial change inside a quotation is exactly what R2-1-LAW 4 forbids, and it is the same class as PASS-3 D-8's bracketed `h[onour]`, cured at §C.I in this round |
| **D · label STRUCK with its source** | **1** | §7's cadence disclosure quoted **`conformance/PASS-1/RULINGS.md`** as authority — the R2-1d shape, recurring inside the round that closed it. Quotation marks and attribution struck; the disclosure restated in F.W3's own voice over the banked `fr-NotationPills.md:35` fact (§7) |
| **E · the word is a SUBJECT-FACT, not a provenance label** | **8** | *"20 lines of result-row markup duplicated verbatim"* · *"verbatim forks"* · *"Law (verbatim, binding)"* and five siblings: the word describes the SUBJECT TREE or a law's binding force, carries no quoted span, and is a claim measurable at the tree (g3's `wc -l` pair, g1's 48/40/8 block census). **Classified here rather than re-worded**, except one — `fr-BasisSelector M-10`'s `basisLabels` clone, re-spelled **byte-identical** because that line sat beside a real quotation and the two readings were adjacent |

**THE STANDING RULE THIS FILE NOW OBEYS (R3-3.10, adopted in terms).** **No ⟨cmd⟩ whose output is a COUNT of a live sibling spec or of this file itself may be banked here as a gate operand.** Sibling and self receipts state **classifications, set-membership, or `grep -o` word-output** — forms that survive the next edit. This round converted five such receipts in place (§X.1-v4 item 1's four negatives · §X.1-v4 item 3's `.d` membership · §5a split (5)'s two admin authorings · §7's rulings-file homes), and each replacement probe is **scoped so that it cannot match the prose that cites it** (R3-3.7) — the failure mode where a receipt invalidates itself the instant it is written down, which is how round 2's own `L-26` control died.
